- **temporal-genomics-circadian-rhythms**: Detects circadian and ultradian rhythms in time-series omics data using CosinorPy cosinor models, MetaCycle (JTK_CYCLE, ARSER), and RAIN non-parametric tests. Fits cosine models to estimate phase and amplitude, tests rhythmicity significance at pre-specified periods. Use when testing for 24-hour or other known-period oscillations in circadian, feeding-fasting, or light-dark cycle experiments. Not for unknown-period discovery (see temporal-genomics/periodicity-detection). ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/temporal-genomics-circadian-rhythms) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **temporal-genomics-periodicity-detection**: Discovers periodic signals of unknown period in time-series omics data using Lomb-Scargle periodograms (scipy), autocorrelation, and wavelet time-frequency decomposition (pywt). Identifies dominant frequencies, handles irregularly sampled data, and detects transient periodicity. Use when searching for periodic patterns of unknown period length, analyzing cell cycle oscillations, or processing unevenly spaced time-series. Not for testing known 24-hour rhythms (see temporal-genomics/circadian-rhythms). ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/temporal-genomics-periodicity-detection) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **temporal-genomics-temporal-clustering**: Clusters genes by temporal expression profile shape using Mfuzz soft clustering, TCseq, and DEGreport degPatterns. Groups co-regulated genes into shared trajectory patterns via fuzzy c-means or hierarchical approaches. Use when categorizing temporally dynamic genes into response groups or identifying co-expression modules across time points. Requires temporally variable genes identified first (see differential-expression/timeseries-de). ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/temporal-genomics-temporal-clustering) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **temporal-genomics-temporal-grn**: Infers dynamic gene regulatory networks from bulk time-series expression data using Granger causality (statsmodels), dynGENIE3 (Extra-Trees on ODE-derived expression derivatives), and dynamic Bayesian networks (bnlearn). Identifies time-delayed regulatory relationships and tracks network rewiring across conditions. Use when inferring causal regulatory relationships from bulk temporal expression data or detecting TF influence propagation over time. Not for static co-expression networks (see gene-regulatory-networks/coexpression-networks). ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/temporal-genomics-temporal-grn) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **temporal-genomics-trajectory-modeling**: Models continuous temporal trajectories from bulk or time-resolved omics data using generalized additive models (mgcv), spline regression, and changepoint detection (segmented, ruptures). Fits smooth gene expression curves and tests trajectory differences between conditions. Use when fitting non-linear temporal models to bulk time-series data or comparing developmental trajectories across conditions. Not for single-cell pseudotime (see single-cell/trajectory-inference). ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/temporal-genomics-trajectory-modeling) · ★104 · 2 copies)

([remodlai/skills-temporal](https://github.com/remodlai/skills-temporal/tree/HEAD/temporal-ts-entity-pattern))
- **temporal-ts-failure-detection**: Use when configuring failure detection, timeouts, retries, heartbeats, or error handling in Temporal TypeScript SDK workflows and activities. Covers ApplicationFailure, retry policies, activity and workflow timeouts, heartbeat checkpointing, and patterns for deliberately failing workflows. Applies to both self-hosted Temporal and Temporal Cloud deployments. ([remodlai/skills-temporal](https://github.com/remodlai/skills-temporal/tree/HEAD/temporal-ts-failure-detection))
- **temporal-ts-integrations**: Use when integrating AI frameworks with the Temporal TypeScript SDK, including the Vercel AI SDK integration for durable LLM calls, configuring Workers with the AiSDKPlugin, using temporalProvider for model calls inside Workflows, providing tools to AI agents via Activities, integrating MCP servers with TemporalMCPClient, and building durable AI agents. Covers the full integration lifecycle from package installation through MCP server connectivity. ([remodlai/skills-temporal](https://github.com/remodlai/skills-temporal/tree/HEAD/temporal-ts-integrations))
- **temporal-ts-interceptors**: Use when implementing, registering, or debugging Temporal TypeScript SDK interceptors for workflows, activities, or clients. Covers inbound and outbound interceptor patterns, OpenTelemetry tracing setup, authorization guards, activity logging, registration on Workers and Clients, and the workflow-isolate constraint for workflow interceptors. ([remodlai/skills-temporal](https://github.com/remodlai/skills-temporal/tree/HEAD/temporal-ts-interceptors))
- **temporal-ts-local-setup**: Use when setting up a local Temporal TypeScript development environment, creating a new Temporal TS project, configuring Workers, writing Workflows and Activities, connecting a Temporal Client, or troubleshooting connection issues between a TypeScript application and a Temporal Server (local or remote). ([remodlai/skills-temporal](https://github.com/remodlai/skills-temporal/tree/HEAD/temporal-ts-local-setup))
- **temporal-ts-message-passing**: Use when implementing Queries, Signals, and Updates in Temporal TypeScript Workflows. Covers defining message handlers (Query, Signal, Update with validators), sending messages from Clients and other Workflows, Signal-With-Start, Update-With-Start, async handler patterns with workflow.condition and Mutex locks, wait conditions, handler concurrency control, and troubleshooting common message-passing errors. Applies to @temporalio/workflow and @temporalio/client packages. ([remodlai/skills-temporal](https://github.com/remodlai/skills-temporal/tree/HEAD/temporal-ts-message-passing))
- **temporal-ts-namespaces**: Use when working with Temporal Namespaces in the TypeScript SDK, including creating, configuring, and connecting to namespaces for both self-hosted Temporal Service and Temporal Cloud. Covers client connection setup, worker namespace configuration, TLS/mTLS authentication, namespace isolation patterns, and environment-specific namespace strategies. ([remodlai/skills-temporal](https://github.com/remodlai/skills-temporal/tree/HEAD/temporal-ts-namespaces))
- **temporal-ts-nexus**: Use when implementing Temporal Nexus in TypeScript to connect Temporal applications across Namespaces. Covers defining Nexus Service contracts, building synchronous and asynchronous Operation handlers, creating caller Workflows, registering handlers in Workers, configuring Nexus Endpoints, error handling with Nexus exception classes, cancellation patterns, and deploying cross-Namespace Nexus calls on Temporal Cloud with mTLS. ([remodlai/skills-temporal](https://github.com/remodlai/skills-temporal/tree/HEAD/temporal-ts-nexus))
- **temporal-ts-observability**: Use when implementing observability for Temporal TypeScript applications, including metrics emission (Prometheus/OpenTelemetry), distributed tracing with OpenTelemetry interceptors, logging from Workflows and Activities, custom Workflow Sinks, custom loggers (Winston/Datadog), Visibility APIs, and Search Attributes (set, upsert, remove, query). Covers Runtime.install telemetry configuration, the sandboxed Workflow logger vs the Activity context logger, and listing Workflow Executions with List Filters. ([remodlai/skills-temporal](https://github.com/remodlai/skills-temporal/tree/HEAD/temporal-ts-observability))
- **temporal-ts-schedules**: Use when creating, managing, or debugging Temporal Schedules in TypeScript. Covers the ScheduleClient API for creating, updating, pausing, triggering, backfilling, listing, describing, and deleting scheduled Workflow executions. Also covers legacy cron jobs and startDelay for one-time delayed execution. ([remodlai/skills-temporal](https://github.com/remodlai/skills-temporal/tree/HEAD/temporal-ts-schedules))
- **temporal-ts-testing-suite**: Use when writing tests for Temporal TypeScript Workflows and Activities, including unit tests, integration tests, time-skipping tests, mocking Activities, testing heartbeats and cancellation, asserting in Workflow context, and replaying Workflow Event Histories for determinism checks. Covers TestWorkflowEnvironment, MockActivityEnvironment, and Worker replay APIs from @temporalio/testing. ([remodlai/skills-temporal](https://github.com/remodlai/skills-temporal/tree/HEAD/temporal-ts-testing-suite))
- **temporal-ts-timers**: Use when implementing durable timers, sleep-based delays, racing patterns, updatable countdowns, or asynchronous timeout logic in Temporal TypeScript Workflows. Covers sleep(), condition(), Promise.race with timers and signals, Trigger-based patterns, and the UpdatableTimer class. Applies to any Workflow that needs to wait for a duration, race a timer against a signal or activity, or allow external updates to a deadline. ([remodlai/skills-temporal](https://github.com/remodlai/skills-temporal/tree/HEAD/temporal-ts-timers))
- **temporal-ts-versioning**: Use when you need to version Temporal Workflows in TypeScript, patch running Workflow Executions, deprecate patches, perform Workflow cutovers, or set up Worker Versioning. - **ten-step-learning**: 把「十步学习法」（五视角 STORM × 检索练习 × 费曼循环）固化成端到端的学习流水线： 用户给一个主题，自动跑完十步（五视角 STORM → 矛盾图谱 → 综合简报 → 同行评审自检 → 资源筛选 → 学习阶梯 → 2 小时核心 20% → 自测题库 → 费曼讲解 → 一页速查表）， 产出一个带侧边栏导航的单文件学习 HTML；也可以只跑其中某一步，或在学习后进入 交互模式（考官一问一答测到理解边界 / 费曼复述陪练）。 当用户说"用十步学习法学 X / 系统学一下 X / 十倍速学 X / 用 STORM 学 X / 深度学习某个主题 / 给我做一份 X 的学习资料·学习闭环·学习 HTML / 把 X 从零学到能和专业人士对谈 / 考考我 X / 用费曼法陪我过一遍 X"时， 即使没有明说"十步"或"HTML"，也应使用本 skill。 反触发：① 一句话能答的事实性问题 → 直接回答；② 要做 PPT / 竞品报告 / 正式文档等 特定产物而非"学习一个主题" → 用对应的产物类 skill。 ([li-xisheng/claude-skills](https://github.com/li-xisheng/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/ten-step-learning) · ★2 · 2 copies)
